## 🟢 M10.3 design Joe-LOCKED (J-366) — live-read accepted_tiers (C2) + 3012 reject + mock --tier issuance; dormant claim schemas stay unwired; next-active = Clair runbook

**M10.3 design Joe-LOCKED (J-366, doc-only, no code).** Clair's Phase-0 audit landed (`325afec`, `tasks/M10_3_MOCK_TIER_AUDIT.md` v1.0, grounded @`52897e5`, 5 findings); Chat verified + framed the five shape calls; Joe locked (by-recomms); Chat authored `tasks/M10_3_MOCK_TIER_DESIGN.md` (v1.0 ACTIVE). **Audit headline (3 catches, frame holds):** A1 (load-bearing) C2 needs the issuer record but `validate_assertion` sees only `trusted_issuers: HashSet<String>` (URIs); A2 (load-bearing) empty `accepted_tiers` must mean unrestricted or C2 regresses M10.2; A4 (D-065) `Tier2/3/4Claims` are `#[cfg(test)]`, no production reader — per-tier claims actually seen = tier integer + TTL + descriptor. **Five decisions LOCKED:** **D1** C2 = add `AuthModuleRegistry::accepted_tiers_by_issuer()` + an `AssertionPolicy` field, **live-read at the gate** beside `trusted_issuers` (~`app.rs:2895`); C2 inserts after Step 1 (`registration.rs:218`), **distinct** from node-wide `tier ≥ required_tier` Step 4 (`registration.rs:230`) — `accepted_tiers` finally enforcement-bearing (M10.2-A1 come due). **D2** empty `accepted_tiers` = **unrestricted** (restrictive-only; C2 invisible at the empty/T1 baseline; empty-baseline invariant byte-for-byte). **D3** reject code **3012 `assertion_tier_unauthorized`** (new auth-module-band slot; distinct from 3030 `tier_mismatch`). **D4** mock issuance = tier + grounded TTL (T2=365/T3=180/T4=90 via `ttl_days`) + descriptor (`module_kind: mock` + tier-appropriate `erasability`, **T4 retained**); the dormant `Tier2/3/4Claims` schemas **NOT populated** (no production reader = theater) — **narrows C3 honestly** (witness the tier-integer gates + TTLs; richer schemas = flagged future). **D5** CLI = a flag on the existing `issue` (`issue --tier <N>`; N=1 reference default, N∈{2,3,4} auto-mock); D-092 not triggered. **Out:** dormant claim schemas (not wired); MP-F13 → M10.4; MP-F6 + MP-C-06/MP-C-16 → M10.5; erasure *enforcement* (D3-gated); mock-refusing node behaviour (flagged). **Witnesses (RED-on-revert):** mock `issue --tier 2|3|4` accepted by a trusting node; C2 scope (T2-issuer's T3 rejected w/ 3012, T2 accepted); empty-baseline invariant; Thread participation tier-gate fires (`runtime.rs:1503`); `module_kind: mock` populated. **Canonical flips (D-074):** design doc NEW (v1.0); this PLAY; `docs/ROADMAP.md` v3.55→v3.56 (M10 detail + markers; dates → 2026-06-14); JOURNAL J-366. No DECISIONS change (arc-local, D-069). (Audit `325afec` committed-not-pushed; rides this bridge's push.) **Next-active: Clair** — author `tasks/M10_3_MOCK_TIER_IMPL.md` (the `accepted_tiers_by_issuer` + `AssertionPolicy` field + the C2 check/3012 + the mock `--tier` path + the dormant-gate witnesses), confirming §3 groundings + §5 details to file:line → implement → Chat doc-bridge → close → M10.4. mock = real software, never a deployable trust anchor); "refuse mock in production" = flagged future. **Out:** MP-F13 → M10.4; MP-F6 fold + MP-C-06/MP-C-16 re-run → M10.5; erasure *enforcement* (D3-gated); mock-refusing node behaviour (flagged). **Phase-0 must ground (brief §3):** the `accepted_tiers` field (`module_registry.rs:59`) + the live-read seam + the C2 insert point vs the node-wide `required_tier` check (distinctness, no double-reject); the Arc-E Thread participation tier-gate; per-tier claims/TTL on `TrustClaims` (T1→T4 diffs); the mock issue surface (`issue_tier1()` → `--tier <N>` + tier-appropriate erasability); the D-088 erasure touch-point (issuance-side only; ground where enforcement *would* attach without building it). C2 the new `xgen-auth-module` member: `issue_tier1()` self-signs a T1 `TrustAssertion` + populates the M10.1 descriptor (`module_kind: reference` + `module_policy.erasability`); offline `keygen`/`issue` CLI (no endpoint). ## 🟢 M10.2 design Joe-LOCKED (J-363) — offline-token issuance + live-read registry→policy + config-seed; accepted_tiers → M10.3; next-active = Clair runbook

**M10.2 design Joe-LOCKED (J-363, doc-only, no code).** Clair's Phase-0 audit landed (`629b30c`, `tasks/M10_2_REFERENCE_BINARY_AUDIT.md` v1.0, grounded @`a86db48`, 5 findings); Chat verified + framed the four shape calls; Joe locked (by-recomms); Chat authored `tasks/M10_2_REFERENCE_BINARY_DESIGN.md` (v1.0 ACTIVE). **Audit headline:** the trust-source change is a **single seam** — one `validate_assertion` caller (`registration.rs:486` via `accept_registration`), one node path (`app.rs:2842`); gate trust = startup config snapshot (`app.rs:745–749`); the binary is an **offline signer** (`endpoint_url` = test-probe only, node verifies offline). Spec-aligned (§3016 step-1 / §3.8.7 = the registry is the registration store). **Four decisions LOCKED:** **D1** issuance = offline-signed token (binary signs a T1 `TrustAssertion`, issuer `AuthModuleXgid::from_pubkey().to_string()`, identity attaches to register, node verifies offline; no endpoint; challenge/response future). **D2** trust source = `AuthModuleRegistry` **live-read**; the `Arc<Mutex<>>` moves to **`run_node` top-level** (shared gate + CRUD, first runtime consumer, **structurally closes AMR-D1**); revoke bites immediately. **D3** config `trusted_auth_modules` **bootstrap-seeds** the registry: **add-only + idempotent + revoke-wins**; prime invariant empty+empty = today byte-for-byte (Fork 1 floor untouched). **D4** `accepted_tiers` enforcement **deferred to M10.3** (nothing higher to gate until the T2–T4 mock; narrows M10-A-02 *timing* only, direction intact; M10.3 owns it — recorded). **D5** binary = pure offline signer (M10-A-06): reuses `keypair.rs`, own keypair, signs T1 as itself, **populates the M10.1 descriptor** (`module_kind: reference` + `module_policy.erasability`), operator registers via CRUD, no manifest. **Honesty (audit A5):** `SignedPrimitive` is concept-not-trait; T1 = proof-of-key-possession. **Witnesses (RED-on-revert):** end-to-end accept (extends `non_local_registration_with_valid_assertion_accepted`); live revoke rejects without restart; config-seed honoured/idempotent/revoke-wins; empty-baseline invariant. **Canonical flips (D-074):** design doc NEW (v1.0); this PLAY; `docs/ROADMAP.md` v3.52→v3.53; JOURNAL J-363. No DECISIONS change (arc-local, D-069). **Next-active: Clair** — author `tasks/M10_2_REFERENCE_BINARY_IMPL.md` (the binary crate + trust-source rewire + registry relocation + config-seed + the 4 witnesses), confirming §3 groundings + §5 details to file:line → implement → Chat doc-bridge → close. One detail to surface if non-obvious: the live-read shape at the gate (§5). No code until the runbook lands. **Entry (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-363 → `tasks/M10_2_REFERENCE_BINARY_DESIGN.md` → `tasks/M10_2_REFERENCE_BINARY_AUDIT.md`.** Not pushed — Joe pushes.

the synthetic test issuer it replaces + the `TrustAssertion` shape); the Call-1b trust-source seam (`app.rs:746` config → `AssertionPolicy.trusted_issuers` `registration.rs:200` → read the registry + where the startup seed lands); `AuthModuleRecord`/`endpoint_url` semantics (does the node *call* the module or only verify the presented assertion's signature — offline signer vs live endpoint); the keypair/signing surface (`SignedPrimitive`, `AuthModuleXgid`/D-083); what "Tier-1 verification" means in code (likely proof-of-key-possession); confirm the binary populates the M10.1 descriptor (`module_kind: reference` + `module_policy.erasability`). D-065/D-078: if grounding contradicts a locked call, surface + re-lock. **Canonical flips (D-074):** brief NEW (v1.0); this PLAY; `docs/ROADMAP.md` v3.51→v3.52 (M10 detail M10.2-open annotation + markers); JOURNAL J-362. ## 🟢 MP-R1 — FIX PHASE (loop-to-green, D10): MP-F1b ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (J-333, Design Z: parties-set + identity-replicate hook; F-3 intact, no skip) — §3.2 "no new send code" falsified mid-arc by the live two-node witness (3rd arc this loop); MP-C-07 cross-node harness-green-with-boundary; invariant E promoted D-091; next-active = Clair (the 4 thin verbs)

**Loop-to-green (MP-R1-D10): MP-F2 ✅ (J-324) → MP-F3 ✅ (J-326) → MP-F1a ✅ (J-328) → MP-F4 ✅ (J-331) → MP-F1b ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (J-333) → remaining: the 4 thin verbs → R1 rerun.** **MP-F1b ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (Clair, code + arc docs, commit `9b4ab8b`; this entry = Chat doc-only follow).** The arc did NOT go straight design→ship: at C2 the **live two-node MP-C-07 witness falsified the J-332 design's §3.2 premise** ("the moment federation_nodes is populated the existing push path federates both directions, no new send code") — C1 (helper + 4 apply-site hooks + 4 units) was green in isolation but the witness stayed RED (bob's join applied on B, alice's view on A stayed `{alice}`). Surfaced, not worked around (D-065); grounded empirically (file-logged member→home diagnostic + the MP-C-02 baseline contrast). **Two receiving-side gaps + a third timing dependency, one root cause (a DM federates *late*):** (1) **F-3 bootstrap chicken-and-egg** — a pushed DM join hits the receiving node's F-3 gate requiring the pusher already in `federation_nodes`, but the join is what would populate it; the drain it relies on never fires for a DM (no `federation_add`); (2) **creator's pre-join message can't push** — a3 has `federation_nodes={A}` at send, and the one-shot initiate catch-up streams on `shared_spaces`, never re-streaming on a later set change (Option C grounded **absent**); (3) **replication race** — the helper can't resolve a party's home until that record replicates, and didn't re-fire on identity-replicate. **The F1B-D8 spine came back falsified:** the Phase-0 premise "the applier re-checks 2-party on the federation path" is **false** — `apply_join` open-joins (J-275), no DM 2-party/join-cap gate anywhere → an unconditional F-3 skip would be a **hole** (3rd-party injected open-join). So F1B-D8 = the finding that drove Z, NOT a skip shipped. **Design Z Joe-LOCKED + shipped (F-3 fully intact, no skip):** populate `federation_nodes` from **parties = members ∪ pending invitees** (for a DM that's exactly the 2 seeded parties from create) → the bootstrap join passes F-3 with no skip (a non-party's node never enters the set → F-3 still blocks 3rd parties, *no hole*, proven by the hole-closed unit); alice's a3 pushes immediately (gap-2 closed via the existing push path, no backfill); + a `repopulate_dm_federation_after_identity` hook fired from the identity-replicate handler re-populates + **drains the F-3-held join** for a late-arriving record (replication-race fix). **D-076 discharged by inheritance** (the drain reuses `drain_pending_by_federation_relationship` verbatim — same hook, one more trigger, `peer_node_id=None`, no new ordering decision); **no empty-set instant** (`apply_join` remove+insert are one apply). X (party-gated skip + backfill) + Option C (auto sync — mechanism absent) rejected. **Invariant E amended (members → parties) + promoted → D-091** (D-069 at close): "a DM's federation set is exactly its parties' home nodes; no other node receives DM content" — refines D-075. **Verification:** build 0 + clippy clean (default + `--all-features`); xgen-core **689/0** (6 units incl. Z-bootstrap-passes-F3 + hole-closed-3rd-party-blocked), xgen-node 286/0; `MP-C-07` cross-node flipped KNOWN-FAIL → **harness-green-with-boundary** (a3+b4 converge A↔B, **stable ×3**); **RED-on-revert genuine** (neuter Z population → empty set → join F-3-held → diverges → RED); **no production witness claimed** (F1B-D4). **Honest boundary:** converges in the harness (G-6 pre-seeds); production DM to a not-yet-discoverable counterparty waits on the routed **F1B-D5 "production identity→home-node discovery"** arc (placed on the ROADMAP horizon at this close; F1b routes, does not build). **Breadcrumb:** late-federating relationships need explicit relationship-formation plumbing — trace what the *receiving* node does with a pushed bootstrap event, not just the sending side (sibling to the MP-F4 design-time-probe breadcrumb; live net caught it, 3rd arc this loop). **DECISIONS change: +D-091.** `tasks/MP_findings.md` v1.6→v1.7 (MP-F1b → Z-shipped/CLOSED); `docs/tests/MULTIPARTY_TEST_MATRIX.md` v1.6→v1.7 (MP-C-07 row); arc docs AUDIT / DESIGN (v1.1) / IMPL (v1.1) → COMPLETED. ROADMAP v3.22→v3.23. **Commit order (standing): Clair's code FIRST, then Chat's doc-bridge.** **Next-active: Clair — the 4 thin verbs** (`ban` [+MP-A-14] / `room_update` / `thread`×3 / `create-space --auth-tier`; each a small xgen-client production arc over an existing core builder, own per-arc discipline) → R1 rerun. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-333 → `tasks/MP_F1B_DM_FEDERATION_DESIGN.md` §9 (Design Z) → `tasks/MP_findings.md` v1.7 (MP-F1b) → `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_DESIGN.md` §11 (D10).** This doc set not pushed — Joe pushes.

## 🟢 MP-R1 — FIX PHASE (loop-to-green, D10): MP-F4 ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (J-331, Option C: frontier anchor + A1 keying) — A1-alone falsified mid-arc by a live-net probe (MP-F3 pattern), re-locked C, root cause = multi-leaf-frontier client bug in get_dag_tips; MP-C-07-LOCAL flipped to 2-party convergence; next-active = Clair (MP-F1b, after Chat's (iii)/A–E/gate-B re-supply)

**Loop-to-green (MP-R1-D10): MP-F2 ✅ (J-324) → MP-F3 ✅ (J-326) → MP-F1a ✅ (J-328) → MP-F4 ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (J-331) → remaining: MP-F1b (facet-1, iii) + 4 thin verbs → R1 rerun.** **MP-F4 ✅ SHIPPED + CLOSED (Clair, code + arc docs, commit bc057f8; Chat doc-only follow this entry).** The arc did NOT go straight design→ship: implementation **falsified the J-330-locked A1-as-sufficient** (the MP-F3/J-326 pattern again) — Clair built A1 (sound keying) then ran an empirical probe before claiming convergence: `space_join_id < room_join_id = false → bob in_space=true, in_room=FALSE`. **Why A1-alone fails:** `apply_join`'s room-level branch guards on space membership (`!members.contains_key` → `NotASpaceMember`, state.rs:988) and `fold_skipping` is single-pass in `topological_sort` order (tie-breaks concurrent siblings lexicographically by event_id); A1 separates the keys but does **not order** the joins — room-join sorts first → guard fails → dropped (~50% by hash). No state-key scheme orders an order-sensitive guard under arbitrary fold order. **Root cause re-grounded (sharper than J-330):** a room-join causally depends on its space-join (the guard), but the client emitted them **concurrent** → dependency never in the DAG. Two compounding defects: (1) **missing causal edge** (client `ops::join`) — the driver; (2) **room-agnostic key conflation** (node `state_key`) — the A1 target. The audit's §2.3 "trace off the critical path" was **wrong, on it.** `get_dag_tips` (batch.rs) returned a **single tip (topo-last), not the DAG frontier** → in MP-C-07-LOCAL alice's pre-join message `a3` and bob's space-join `b2` are sibling leaves, so bob's room-join `b3` anchored to whichever sorts last (often `a3`) → concurrent with `b2` → dropped. **Option C Joe-LOCKED + shipped (anchor-primary + A1-retained):** the **anchor is the finding-closer** — `get_dag_tips` now returns the **true DAG frontier** (all leaves, sorted, capped at `MAX_PREV_EVENTS`=10) → room-join causally descends from its space-join → guard passes (strictly-more-correct; linear DAGs frontier==single-tip → inert; a general client-anchoring fix affecting all callers join/leave/send). **A1 retained** as latent-conflation defense (scope-aware membership `state_key` across join/leave/kick; invite/ban/eject/unban space-level; D-077-forward — two-device / federation-reorder). B (fold-order rule) rejected (D-076-sensitive, wrong layer); A (anchor-only) rejected (discards sound A1). **Verification (actual):** build 0 + clippy clean (default `--all-features` + `--features harness-control`); xgen-core 683/0 (A1 units + 4 convergence witnesses), xgen-node 286/0, xgen-client 103/0 + integration; heavy `MP-C-07-LOCAL` flipped delivery-only → **2-party DM message convergence** (a3+b4 on Node A) PASS, MP-C-01 contrast PASS (concurrency-robust); **all-callers / D-076 / D-077 sweep discharged green** (no single-tip-dependent test broke; multi-parent `prev_events` already legal; no wire/persistence/reason change). **Sensitivity (honest, D-065):** clean RED-on-revert = the unit probe (A1-alone → dropped, deterministic); the heavy MP-C-07-LOCAL revert is fold-order-flaky (~50% by hash), recorded as such. **Honest boundary:** MP-F4 closes **single-node** convergence; the **federated** MP-C-07 stays KNOWN-FAIL → MP-F1b. **Breadcrumb (in the corrected design):** membership/resolution arcs warrant an empirical fold-order probe at *design* time — second arc the live net caught a fold/order subtlety reasoning missed (MP-F3, now MP-F4); surface-and-route worked both times. ## 🟢 MP-R1 — FIX PHASE (loop-to-green, D10): MP-F4 design Joe-LOCKED (J-330, F4-D1..D5) — A1 + the join/leave/kick room-scope widening locked; spine proof = guard+cascade complementarity (ban-evasion closed); design 861b4ab; D-076 re-proven set = 3 pairs; next-active = Clair (MP-F4 runbook)

**Loop-to-green (MP-R1-D10): MP-F2 ✅ (J-324) → MP-F3 ✅ (J-326) → MP-F1a ✅ (J-328) → MP-F4 Phase-0 + A1-lock (J-329) → MP-F4 design Joe-LOCKED (J-330) → remaining: MP-F4 runbook→impl→close, then MP-F1b (facet-1, iii) + 4 thin verbs → R1 rerun.** **MP-F4 design Joe-LOCKED (Clair authored + committed 861b4ab, `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_DESIGN.md`; F4-D1..D5, arc-local D-069).** **F4-D1** key transform: membership key stays `membership:{space}:{id}` when `room_id` empty (space-level), becomes `membership:{space}:room:{room}:{id}` when populated (room-level) — one edit site (state_key.rs) + one helper. **F4-D2 the spine (cross-scope-conflict proof, proven not assumed):** a space-level removal (ban/kick/leave) dominates a room-level join across ALL orderings via guard+cascade complementarity — removal-before-join → `apply_join`'s room-level guard (`!members.contains_key` → `NotASpaceMember`) no-ops the join; join-before-removal → the removal cascade (`room.members.remove(id)`) undoes the join's insert; both → id ∉ members ∉ room, no shared key. §5.6 pins concurrent-ban-vs-room-join to "banned, in no room" under every permutation → **no ban-evasion opened.** **A1-scope sharpening Joe-LOCKED (Clair-surfaced, D-065):** J-329's "join/leave" is completed to **scope-aware across join/leave/kick** — all three have room-level applier branches and room-leave is live (`ops::leave --room`, `KickArgs.room`); room-scoping only `join` would leave room-kick on the old key → a NEW room-kick-vs-room-join non-convergence. Correctness requirement, not scope-creep; invite/ban/eject/unban stay always-space-level. **D-076 re-proven set = THREE pairs** (the widening enlarges the surface), each genuinely RED on revert: cross-scope removal×room-join (F4-D2) + room-kick×room-join + room-leave×room-join (the two same-key room-level pairs the widening keeps conflicting). Space-level keys stay byte-identical; only wrongly-conflated facts separate. **F4-D3:** all 5 readers consume `StateKey` opaquely, never serialized → no migration / no wire / no reason-string change; appliers untouched. **F4-D4 witness:** MP-C-07-LOCAL delivery-only → 2-party convergence (a3+b4), RED on revert (confirms a3 lands post-MP-F1a). **F4-D5:** F1b cross-link flagged not merged; federated MP-C-07 stays KNOWN-FAIL. **D-077:** MP-C-01 stays green + becomes robust (was luck-dependent). Audit §2.3 proximate-cause (staleness vs `get_dag_tips`) off the critical path — A1 fixes the domain regardless. No DECISIONS change (F4-D# arc-local, D-069). Design doc → v1.1 to lock the join/leave/kick widening language (Clair, riding the runbook commit). ROADMAP v3.19→v3.20. **Commit order (standing): Clair's code/arc-doc FIRST, then Chat's doc-bridge.** **Next-active: Clair — author `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_IMPL.md`** (runbook; the three-pair D-076 witness set is a hard deliverable) → implement → close. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-330 → `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_DESIGN.md` (F4-D1..D5 + §5.6 spine) → `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_AUDIT.md` v1.1 → `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_DESIGN.md` §11 (D10).** This doc set not pushed — Joe pushes.

Different code surfaces (F4 = node-side membership resolution; F1b = `federation_nodes` population) → no rework risk. **MP-F4 Phase-0 SHIPPED + pushed (Clair, 71e8b72; `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_AUDIT.md` v1.1) — GAP CONFIRMED, moderate.** Mechanism grounded precisely: `state_key_for_event` (state_key.rs:48) keys membership room-agnostically (`membership:{space}:{sender}`) → a space-join + room-join by one identity collapse onto one key; the drop is `derive_resolved`'s `frontier_of` (derive.rs:179) — concurrent siblings (both anchored to `[invite]`) form a size-2 frontier → conflict → resolve() (Layer-5c) elects one, drops the other → member-of-Space-not-room → message rejected step-11 NotARoomMember. It is **resolution dropping an admitted fact** → the fix is in keying. **Honest finding (D-065):** the routed "bootstrap re-issues to an already-member" premise (which A2 targets) is **not reproducible from the static node path** (`collect_invite_bootstrap` authorizes on `pending_invites`, cleared by the space-join's `apply_join`); the true proximate concurrency source needs a runtime trace, but that is **off the critical path because A1 closes the finding regardless.** MP-C-01 survives the same pattern today **by luck** (its room-join chains causally). **F4-A = A1 Joe-LOCKED — room-scope the membership `state_key`** (room-level join/leave keyed with a room dimension; space-level ops stay room-agnostic) over A2 (gate `get_invite_bootstrap` to non-members) / A3: single no-drift location (one function; all 5 production readers — runtime.rs:673, conflict.rs ×3, derive.rs ×2, xgen-client ai_service.rs:544 R2-F01 gate — consume `StateKey` opaquely; zero serde/persist/wire → no migration); robust to the proximate-cause ambiguity A2 depends on; closes the **latent room-agnostic conflation** (D-077 forward — A2 would leave it live for any future concurrent membership event). **Design spine (the load-bearing obligation):** A1 makes **D-076 non-trivial** (unlike delivery-only F2/F3) — once room-level and space-level membership occupy different key-groups, a **space-level removal (ban/kick/leave) must still dominate a room-level join across all orderings**; Clair's static read says the `apply_join` guard + ban/kick room cascade carry it, but per principle this is **proven, not assumed** — it is the spine of `tasks/MP_F4_DM_MEMBERSHIP_DESIGN.md`. **Witness flip:** MP-C-07-LOCAL delivery-only → 2-party message convergence (a3 + b4), genuinely RED on revert (first DM-message convergence assertion; confirm alice's a3 also lands post-MP-F1a). **F1b cross-link:** different code, flagged for F1b Phase-0, not merged. Federated MP-C-07 (mp_r1_c4) stays KNOWN-FAIL (MP-F1b). No DECISIONS change (F4-A# arc-local, D-069). `tasks/HANDOFF_MP_F4.md` → COMPLETED (consumed). ## 🟢 MP-R1 — FIX PHASE (loop-to-green, D10): MP-F1 SPLIT (J-327) — F1a (delivery) arc shovel-ready + C1 GREENLIT; F1b (cross-node DM) resolution (iii) Joe-LOCKED (A–E), Phase-0 next (gate-B first). MP-F2 ✅ (J-324) + MP-F3 ✅ (J-326) shipped. next-active = Clair (MP-F1a C1)

**Loop-to-green (MP-R1-D10): MP-F2 ✅ (J-324) → MP-F3 ✅ (J-326) → MP-F1 split (J-327): F1a in-build (C1), F1b queued ((iii)/A–E locked) → 4 thin verbs → R1 rerun-to-all-green-except-C-06.** **MP-F1 grounded + split (Clair, empirical).** MP-F1 is **two independent problems**: **facet-2 (delivery)** — `create_dm_space` sends 3 events fire-and-forget then goodbye; the RST discards events 2–3; the client never awaits the D-070 `EventAccepted` it already receives (await-ack prototype landed all 3) → a client-send bug. **facet-1 (cross-node DM convergence)** — does NOT share that cause; with every event forced to land, membership still diverges. **Empirically confirmed:** every DM event push logs `federation_nodes=0` (all 7 attempts, both nodes) — a DM Space has `dm_constraints_active=true` → `apply_federation_add` rejects (`DmFederationNotAllowed`, state.rs:660) → `federation_nodes` never populated → `apply_federation_push` early-returns → nothing federates. **The split (Joe-locked):** **MP-F1a** = the await-`EventAccepted` delivery fix (carried to runbook this session); **MP-F1b** = the cross-node DM federation tension (DMs constrained from federation by design 3.16.1/M8.5 vs MP-C-07 expecting convergence) — a design call, not a patch. **facet-1 resolution Joe-LOCKED = (iii) membership-driven DM federation** (over (i) rescope-the-test / (ii) loosen-the-gate): leave `DmFederationNotAllowed` intact (no third-party node gets DM content); add a separate rule — a DM's `federation_nodes` = exactly its current members' home nodes, populated at membership-apply time. Symmetric, neither party privileged; respects independence + keeps 3.16.1 privacy. MP-C-07 NOT rescoped — a real code arc. **A–E sub-locks:** A populate at membership-apply (not `apply_federation_add`); **B home-node resolvability = the feasibility gate — F1b Phase-0 proves it empirically FIRST, can kill (iii)**; C leave shrinks the set, historical local copy = standing federated-RTBF, acknowledged-not-solved in-arc; D `dm_space_create` rides the G-6 handshake, the set carries the rest, DAG dedup (MP-F3) covers overlap; E new invariant "a DM's federation set is exactly its members' home nodes" = **DECISIONS candidate, NOT yet promoted** (D-069). **MP-F1a arc shovel-ready (Phase-0 → design → runbook; F1A-D1..D6 locked).** Principle: *a verb does not return/close until each event it sent is node-confirmed (or its timeout policy fires)* — the client finally consumes the D-070 ack (app.rs comment "per-verb submit-and-await behaviour is deferred" = this work). **F1A-D1 (F-1) scope = (c) all 9 sending ops** route through the confirm helper (durable client-send discipline; the 4 thin verbs inherit it; `create_dm_space` is the only current multi-event op (3), the rest single-event green only by harness timing-luck). **F1A-D2 (F-4)** `Connection::send_event_confirmed(event, timeout) -> Result<EventConfirm, TransportError>`, `EventConfirm = Accepted | Rejected{code,reason} | TimedOut`, helper observes / op applies policy, `event_id`-matched (D-067 no-drift). **F1A-D3 (F-2)** Accepted and Rejected both = node took it → proceed; a `Rejected` of the verb's own event → verb error. **F1A-D4 (F-5+sizing) timeout = op-class split, discriminator = whether await-ack already disambiguated the timeout:** chain → mid-chain timeout is provably-lost (predecessor was acked-present) → abort + `Err`; single-event → lost-vs-HeldPending ambiguous → warn + proceed. Timeout reuses `[sync].completion_timeout_seconds` (5s, D-067). **F1A-D5 (F-3)** node-wire-neutral; the single-event silent-HeldPending residue = named silent-discard shape (D-065/B3); **"HeldPending positive visibility" routed — not solved, not folded**. **F1A-D6 (D-076) discharged** (client send-pacing only; events byte-identical; no ordering surface). Empirical correction: the DM auto-invite is *Accepted* (DAG-valid; DM-constraint reject is an internal no-op swallowed) → all 3 chain events confirm Accepted. **Runbook (`tasks/MP_F1A_SEND_CONFIRM_IMPL.md`):** C1 helper + 5 units over an in-process WS pair (`duplex`+`client_async`/`accept_async`) → C2 ops retrofit (9 ops) + facet-2 witness (stub-WS policy tests + single-node `MP-C-07-LOCAL` mptest RED→GREEN, isolates facet-2) → C3 doc-only close. **Two confirm-at-pickup flags (non-blocking):** CP-1 single-event `Rejected→Err` is a real contract change (surfaces rejections to batch ops, the MP-R1-D9/J-081 §5 gap — any test relying on a swallowed reject is a finding); CP-2 if the xgen-core WS-pair scaffolding is awkward, fall back to C2 stub tests. **CP-3 (Appendix F / D-028 — Chat-seat, flagged J-327):** F1a changes command exit/contract semantics (create-dm-space fallible on confirm-timeout; single-event `Rejected→Err` now surfaces), NOT CLI syntax — so Appendix F's exit-code + batch-reply-schema sections + affected usage examples update at **C3** (Chat owns the spec doc); C2 checks whether any `main.rs` per-verb doc comment shifts (D-028 lockstep). This is a hard C3 deliverable, not optional. **C1 GREENLIT (Joe)** — D-071 runbook-gate satisfied; Clair proceeds to C1 (additive helper + units, no ops touched — lowest-risk slice, reviewable as working code at the C1/C2 boundary). **Honest boundary (D-065):** F1a fixes facet-2's home-node half + the multi-event reliability class; it does NOT flip MP-C-07 green (federated MP-C-07 stays known-FAIL until F1b/(iii)). No DECISIONS change (F1A-D# + F1b A–E arc-local, D-069; invariant E a candidate). Arc docs `tasks/MP_F1A_SEND_CONFIRM_{AUDIT,DESIGN,IMPL}.md` ACTIVE v1.0. Defect: a re-submitted duplicate `event_id` is applied once (DAG/store/disk dedup holds 3 ways) but `dispatch_event` returns `Accepted` → `apply_fanout` re-broadcasts → members receive 2× (mild amplification, no state corruption). **Design Joe-LOCKED (`tasks/MP_F3_DEDUP_REFANOUT_DESIGN.md` v1.0, F3-D1..D5):** D1 dedup check in `dispatch_event` post-`ensure_store`, before `validate_event`, on `store.contains(event_id)` (no-drift D-067; free skip of re-validation); **D2 new unit variant `DispatchOutcome::Duplicate`** (correct inverse of MP-F2's call — MP-F2 *changed* a payload so 1-tuple minimised churn; MP-F3 *adds* a variant so wildcards survive, only 7 production exhaustive arms get a trivial `Duplicate => {}`, compiler-caught — lower churn + more honest than an `Accepted{novel}` flag); D3 `process_inbound` `Duplicate` arm = idempotent `EventAccepted` (truthful — it *was* accepted first time; rejecting a converged event would lie + spuriously error a retrying client) + `FanoutRequest::none()`; D4 `none()` kills local fan-out + federation push together; D5 the §6 side-effect-skip audit (all five re-run effects idempotent/already-fired → early-return loses nothing) is a build-time obligation. **Proof obligation pinned (§5):** MP-A-09 flips from tolerant `n>=1` to **"fanned out exactly once"** — falsifiable, retires the measurement-gap note, turns MP-A-09 into a clean PASS. **D-076 discharged:** duplicate already in store+applied; state is a pure function of the log; drop-vs-idempotent-reapply → identical log → identical convergence; no ordering surface touched. **Two out-of-scope catches (Clair, surfaced-not-chased, design §7):** drained events don't fan out (separate seam); re-submit-while-pending is buffer-not-store (already benign, `PendingBuffer::add` idempotent). Clair corrected her own grep (app.rs:1447 = `match Inbound`, false positive). Suite unchanged (design only). No DECISIONS change (MP-F3-D# arc-local, D-069). Supersedes D8.** The tests were the instrument; the deliverable is a corrected protocol. **BLOCKED-sizing (Clair): 5 THIN / 1 REAL-ARC** — for ban/room-override/thread/auth-tier the whole core path (EventType+builder+applier+gate+M8 convergence) already shipped + is proven; only the client `ops::*` verb is missing. So the hybrid's "don't build UI surface twice" evaporates (the hard part is done) → **loop-to-green wins** (Joe's instinct, vindicated). **Strategy:** (1) fix 3 findings **MP-F2→F3→F1**; (2) build **4 thin verbs** — `ban` (+unblocks MP-A-14) / `room_update` / `thread`×3 / `create-space --auth-tier` param — each a small `xgen-client` PRODUCTION arc over an existing builder, unblocking MP-C-08/09/13 + MP-A-03/14; (3) **DEFER MP-C-06 re-home** — sole real arc (unbuilt `home_changed` broadcast J-278/J-279 + harness keypair-relocation), the one surviving test-debt item → M10/re-home-notify era; (4) re-run R1, loop. **Close criterion (resolved): R1 re-runs all-green EXCEPT MP-C-06.** "Thin" = low-risk shim, NOT zero-process (each verb signs+sends real events → full per-arc discipline). ≈ 3 fixes + 4 verb-arcs + rerun + 1 deferred = weeks not days. **Root cause (Joe's observation, recorded design §11):** the client-verb surface silently lagged the core — each core growth (Arc D/E, ban, auth-tier) stopped at "builder+applier+convergence proven," never adding the verb, because nothing drove it until a test did. **Forward rule: a core addition checks its CLI equivalent at ship time.** **MP-F2 Phase-0 (Clair): GAP CONFIRMED, low-severity** (observability/contract, not security). Root = two-boundary code-drop (NOT wire-shape — the `Error` frame already has `error_code: u32`): `dispatch_event` flattens to `Rejected(String)` via `err.to_string()` (runtime.rs:1086) + `reject_signal` hardcodes 4000 (app.rs:2395). Suite 72/0. No DECISIONS change (MP-R1-D# arc-local, D-069). Design v1.4; ROADMAP v3.13. **Next-active: Clair — author the MP-F2 design** on the §12 forks → Joe-lock → runbook → implement → close. ## 🟢 MP-R1 — design Joe-LOCKED (J-317, 2026-06-07): the deterministic correctness floor; MP-R1-D1..D6 locked — one generic `run_scenario` runner (D1) + the sweep contract for R2/R3 (D2) + manifest `[[clock]]` (D3) + Space-scoped oracle (D4) + 22 scenarios in 4 tranches (D5) + surface-and-route defects (D6); next-active = MP-R1 runbook

**MP-R1 design phase authored + Joe-LOCKED at J-317 (2026-06-07; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_DESIGN.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Locks MP-R1-D1..D6 (arc-local, D-069), grounded against the live harness (`batch.rs::run_actor`, `dial.rs::RoundDial`, `manifest.rs`, `oracle.rs`).** **D1 (F-A) one generic `run_scenario(scenario, dial)` runner, two actor kinds** — reuses the existing per-actor `run_actor` unchanged; the new code is the top orchestrator the Round-0 smokes hand-wired: spawn nodes per topology → establish `[[federation]]` links via the **G-6 bootstrap** (D1a) → spawn a client per actor → attach `EventCollector` per node → drive all actors **concurrently** (required — cross-actor `{{exports}}`/`[[waits]]` resolve only if producers + consumers run together) → settle → oracle. **D1a (G-6, encoded once in the runner):** add-peer each direction (empty spaces) BEFORE any identity registers (so `push_identity_to_peers` replicates it) → register → create Space → re-add-peer naming the Space → `initiate`. Batch actor (→ `run_actor`) vs injector actor (→ raw-wire `injector.rs`) = per-actor dispatch. R1 runs need a `--features harness-control` node build. **D2 (F-B) the sweep contract, locked now so R2/R3 inherit** — `RoundDial` is a single point today; a thin `Sweep { axis, start, step, max, stop_on_fail }` yields a sequence of dials; result = `SweepResult { rungs, break_point }` (a curve, not a bool). The **D-065 distinction is the stop condition:** GREEN → climb; LOGIC-FAULT (non-convergence / lost admitted event / wrong rejection) → stop + route a finding; CEILING (oracle inconclusive + `resource.rs` OOM/RSS-wall/thread-thrash) → stop, tagged **hardware**, NOT a protocol FAIL. R1 = a degenerate single-rung sweep through the SAME type. **D3 (F-C) manifest `[[clock]]` ordered step list** (`{node, op: advance|set, value, after: <export-key|barrier>}`) — a scenario-director task (per-actor JSONL stays actor-driven), each step blocks on `after` via `Registry::wait_for` then sends the F3 verb; unblocks MP-A-01; un-stales `dial.rs` G-2 (`ClockMode::Mock` valid on a harness-control build). **D4 (F-D) Space-scoped oracle as the contract; NO binary change** — the oracle is already Space-scoped (`convergence_verdict(…, space_id)`, `event_ids_for_space`, membership = owner + `(identity,role)` set); every R1 scenario asserts on its own fresh unique Space, never absolute node counts (shared default `spaces_dir` G-4 pollutes those); logic rejections assert the reply error code/category (new small `rejection_category_verdict` helper; data already captured). **D5 (F-E) 22 R1 scenarios in 4 mechanism-grouped tranches** (each a runbook commit, fix→rerun): T1 cross-node cooperative core (MP-C-02 true A↔B / -03 / -07, riskiest path first) · T2 membership-lifecycle (MP-C-01/06/08/09/10/13) · T3 logic-adversarial (MP-A-02/03/04/14/16/17/20, assert code/category) · T4 wire/injector + clock (MP-A-05 re-run /09/10/12/15 + MP-A-01 via `[[clock]]`). **D6 (F-F) defect policy = surface-and-route, not patch** — a real-defect FAIL becomes a routed finding in a NEW `tasks/MP_findings.md`; does NOT block close; the recorded per-scenario result is the deliverable. **Change surface (all in `xgen-mptest` + scenario dirs):** `run_scenario` + G-6 helper; `Sweep`/`SweepResult` + single-rung path; manifest `[[clock]]` + clock-director; `rejection_category_verdict` + un-stale `dial.rs`; the 22 scenario dirs. Untouched: every production crate (findings route out). Heavy entry points stay `#[ignore]`; the fast suite never spawns processes. **Honest boundary (D-065):** R1 proves correctness under NO load — floor, not scale (R2/R3), not coverage. Suite 1271/0/11 (no code). No DECISIONS change (MP-R1-D# arc-local, D-069). ROADMAP v3.06→v3.07. **Next-active: the MP-R1 runbook** (`tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_IMPL.md`) — commit plan = the runner + types (D1/D2/D3/D4 machinery), then the four scenario tranches (D5) → Clair. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-317 → `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_DESIGN.md` §2–§7 → `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_AUDIT.md` §3 → `docs/tests/MULTIPARTY_TEST_MATRIX.md` (R1 set).** Clair stands down until the runbook exists. Not pushed — Joe pushes.

## 🟢 Multiparty-tests OPENED — MP-R1 (deterministic correctness floor): D-071 Phase-0 audit authored (J-316, 2026-06-07) — central build = a general `run_scenario` runner (none exists yet); 6 grounding findings; ladder = continuous sweep within / monotonic climb across rounds; next-active = MP-R1 design

**The unnumbered strategic Multiparty-tests milestone OPENED at J-316 (2026-06-07; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). It RUNS the M9 `xgen-mptest` harness through three monotonically-heavier sub-passes MP-R1 → MP-R2 → MP-R3 (Joe-locked structure + naming). Deliverable: `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_AUDIT.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE) — the D-071 Phase-0 audit for MP-R1.** **MP-R1 = the correctness floor:** light, MockClock, fixed seeds, fix→rerun — proves every logic/wire scenario converges/rejects correctly *before* any load. "Finalized binary" = the convergence/federation/MLS core (M1–M9.2 shipped, = what the matrix exercises); M10/M11 layer on top without touching ordering. **Grounded against live main (§3, six findings):** **G-1 (the crux)** no end-to-end `run_scenario` orchestrator exists — the manifest declares `[[federation]]` links + M9.2 shipped `add-peer`/`initiate`, but nothing wires them; Round-0 hand-coded two smokes (`c5_mp_c_02`, `c5_mp_a_05`). The general runner (spawn topology → establish federation → drive actors w/ exports/waits/barriers → oracle → matrix result) is MP-R1's primary build. **G-2** `dial.rs` is stale pre-M9.2: `ClockMode::Mock` declared inoperable + rejected by `validate()` (+ a stale "initiate does not exist yet" comment) — F3 made Mock operable; R1 un-stales the dial + wires clock mode to the `clock advance`/`set` verbs. **G-3** no clock-control step in the scenario format (manifest/JSONL) — MP-A-01 needs clock-advance *between* commands; net-new. **G-4** state-isolation hazard — spawned nodes default `spaces_dir` to a SHARED `<exe_dir>/spaces` → absolute-state oracles get polluted; both smokes dodged it Space-scoped. **G-5** `residents_per_process` multiplexing unbridged (one process per actor) — R2/R3 prerequisite, recorded. **G-6** federation bootstrap ordering is load-bearing (seed peer → register identity → create Space → re-seed → initiate, J-315) — the runner encodes it canonically. **Forks for MP-R1 design (§4, Joe-lock):** F-A runner shape (rec: one generic `run_scenario`) · **F-B the sweep contract** (rec: a `Sweep` wrapper over `RoundDial` — the dial is a single POINT today, no sweep concept; per-rung oracle = stop condition; locked here so R2/R3 inherit) · F-C scenario clock-control (rec: manifest `[[clock]]` step) · F-D state isolation (rec: Space-scoped oracle as the contract) · F-E the R1 scenario set · F-F defect policy restated. **The ladder (§5, locked shape):** weight is a continuous sweep WITHIN a round (volume walks a sequence, oracle-checked per rung; the break-point IS the deliverable, not bare PASS/FAIL) + a monotonic climb ACROSS rounds toward the ~1,562-process wall. **Mandatory oracle distinction (D-065):** the sweep MUST separate a logic fault (non-convergence / lost admitted event / wrong rejection) from a box-ceiling artifact (OOM / thrash) — else it mislabels "ran out of RAM" as "protocol broke." **R1 scenario set (§6): 22** (9 cooperative + 13 adversarial); 2 already PASS at Round-0 single-node (MP-C-02, MP-A-05) promoted to true cross-node; the other 13 (scale/real-clock/volume/chaos/MLS-epoch) carry an R2/R3 first round = out of MP-R1 scope. **Defect policy (D-065/D-084):** surface-and-route, NOT patch — a FAIL becomes a routed finding (new `tasks/MP_findings.md`) and does not block MP-R1 close; the recorded result is the deliverable. **Honest boundary (D-065):** R1 proves correctness under NO load — not scale, not coverage; a green R1 is a floor, not a robustness claim. Suite 1271/0/11 (no code). No DECISIONS change (MP-R1-D# arc-local, D-069). ROADMAP v3.05→v3.06 (Multiparty-tests 🟡→🟢). **Next-active: MP-R1 design phase** — lock F-A..F-F (F-A runner shape + F-B sweep contract are the crux); ground the runner against `manifest.rs`/`resolve.rs`/`oracle.rs`/`dial.rs` + the F2/F3 smoke invocation patterns; author design → runbook → Clair. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-316 → `tasks/MP_R1_DETERMINISTIC_AUDIT.md` §3+§4 → `docs/tests/MULTIPARTY_TEST_MATRIX.md` (R1 set) → `tasks/M9_findings.md` (F2/F3/F4 now closed/unblocked).** Clair stands down until the MP-R1 runbook exists. ## 🟢 M9.2 — harness-enablement seams (F2/F3/F4): D2 re-opened + corrected (J-314, 2026-06-07) — F2 needs a FederationRegistry upsert, NOT `peer_urls` (Clair-surfaced grounding miss); F3/F4 delivered; next-active = Clair (C1′)

**M9.2-D2 re-opened + corrected at J-314 (2026-06-07; Clair-surfaced, Chat-verified + Joe-locked — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Design → v1.1 (§8), AUDIT → v1.1 (§3.1 erratum), IMPL → v1.1 (§7 addendum).** During implementation Clair surfaced a 🔴 headline finding (D-065, honest-over-fast): **the locked F2 mechanism does not achieve its purpose.** D2 / audit §3 said "seed `peer_urls`, then existing `federation initiate` targets the peer" — **wrong** against live main: `admin_ops::federation_initiate` (admin_ops.rs:1728) reads the **`FederationRegistry`** relationship (`reg.get(peer)` → `rel.peer_url` + `rel.shared_spaces`; **FED_3006** if absent), **never** `peer_urls` (which feeds only identity-replication + a legacy count). Clair implemented add-peer faithfully to the locked (wrong) mechanism, wrote an honest reduced F2 smoke, and surfaced the gap rather than silently redesigning a Joe-locked decision. Chat verified + owned the Phase-0 grounding miss (the audit grounded the `peer_urls` field but never traced `initiate`'s consumer). **D2′ (design v1.1 §8):** `federation add-peer <node_id> <url> [space_id...]` (explicit `shared_spaces`) **upserts a `FederationRelationship` via `FederationRegistry::upsert`** (registry.rs:182): `peer_url = Some(url)`, `shared_spaces` = the args, `state = Active`, placeholder `negotiated_version = "0.1"` + default serialisation + `session_id` + `last_connected = now`; ALSO calls `record_peer_url`. `initiate` reads only `peer_url` + `shared_spaces` (admin_ops.rs:1751/1758) → the placeholders are non-gating (serde round-trip + other registry readers). Then `initiate` dials + replicates; the full F2 smoke passes. **Honest boundary, sharpened (D-065):** add-peer now **fabricates a "pre-established" relationship** (state Active, placeholder negotiated fields) that no real handshake/approval created — a *larger* fabrication than a URL, acceptable ONLY because fenced; it **reinforces** M9.2-D1, and is still NOT production peer-discovery. **Delivered vs outstanding:** F3 (clock advance/set + startup MockClock install; the C2 stash-concrete-`Arc<MockClock>` trap handled exactly) + F4 (xgen-mptest raw malformed-frame client, no production surface) **fully delivered + live-passing**; fence test passes both halves (bonus: F3 over the default binary → UNKNOWN_COMMAND, fence holds at the process boundary). **Only C1′ outstanding.** **Reconciliations:** suite is **1271/0/11** (not "stays 1269/0/8"): +3 ignored = smokes, +2 passed = legit new unit tests (`MockClock::set_now`, default-build fence-absent); the new verbs are **Write-tier, NOT `FEDERATION_VERBS`** (local mutations, not the 180 s handshake tier — Clair's accepted deviation). No DECISIONS change (M9.2-D# arc-local, D-069). ## 🟢 M9.2 — harness-enablement seams (F2/F3/F4): runbook authored (J-313, 2026-06-07) — fence = compile-time `harness-control` feature; F4 drops out of the fence (test-crate-only); next-active = Clair (C1→C2→C3)

**M9.2 design phase authored + Joe-LOCKED at J-313 (2026-06-07; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/M9_2_HARNESS_SEAMS_DESIGN.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Locks M9.2-D1..D5 (arc-local, D-069).** **Grounding confirmed this session (F-B/F-D plumbing):** `mock-clock` is an `xgen-common` cargo feature pulled by xgen-core/xgen-node only under `[dev-dependencies]` — `MockClock` is NOT in a normal binary build; the binary reads time via `runtime.clock().now_utc()` (default `RealClock`) and never calls `set_clock` (test-only seam), so driving a running binary's clock is genuinely new wiring and the fence can pull `mock-clock` into the binary via feature-forwarding. **Locked decisions:** **D1 (the fence, F-D)** — off-by-default cargo feature `harness-control` on xgen-node; every F2/F3 seam `#[cfg(feature = "harness-control")]`-gated (verb registration + dispatch + handler) → a default build physically cannot contain them; plumbing `xgen-node [features] harness-control = ["xgen-common/mock-clock"]`; NOT a runtime dev-flag (the security property is un-buildability in release). **D2 (F2, F-A)** — fenced aicontrol `federation add-peer <node_id> <url>` inserts into `NodeRuntime.peer_urls`, after which existing `federation initiate` targets the now-known peer; no config peer-list. **D3 (F3, F-B)** — fenced aicontrol `clock advance <dur>` / `clock set <rfc3339>` driving the binary's injected `MockClock` via `set_clock` (installed at startup only under the feature; default = RealClock); D1's feature-forward makes MockClock available in the binary. **D4 (F4, F-C)** — test-crate-only raw `tokio-tungstenite` client in `xgen-mptest` writing arbitrary bytes at the node's frame parser; **NO `Connection::send_raw` in any production crate, NO `harness-control` needed** (the parser is in the normal build; a test crate is inherently un-shippable). **F4 therefore drops out of the fence — only F2/F3 sit behind it.** **D5 (F-E)** — one design; runbook = three small commits (C1 feature+add-peer / C2 clock verb + mock-clock-in-binary + startup set_clock / C3 raw client; C3 orthogonal). **Fence guarantee (§4):** a default/release build has no add-peer verb, no clock verb, no MockClock linked — compiled out, not guarded. **Proof obligation:** a test that the verbs are ABSENT in a default build and PRESENT only under `--features harness-control`. **Honest boundaries (D-065):** (1) `add-peer` is a harness seam, NOT production peer-discovery (how real nodes discover/authorize each other is a separate, still-open product question, out of M9.2 scope); (2) the fence is a security property — compile-time makes peer-seeding / clock-tamper un-buildable in release; (3) F4 models a hostile peer, not a compromised honest binary. **Proof plan (§6 — M9.2 proves the seams + the fence, NOT the batteries):** fence test + F2 smoke (two fresh binaries add-peer → initiate → Space replicates) + F3 smoke (clock advance moves now_utc deterministically) + F4 smoke (raw truncated frame rejected at parse, no panic); run `#[ignore]`/out-of-band like M9 Round-0; fast suite stays 1269/0/8. **Change surface:** xgen-node (Cargo `[features]`; fenced verb handlers in `aicontrol.rs` + `app.rs` startup) all `#[cfg(feature="harness-control")]`; xgen-mptest (raw client, C3). Untouched: protocol/validation, ordering, the default binary's behaviour, all other crates. Suite 1269/0/8 (no code). No DECISIONS change (M9.2-D# arc-local). ROADMAP v3.01→v3.02 (M9.2 design Joe-LOCKED). **Runbook authored J-313** (`tasks/M9_2_HARNESS_SEAMS_IMPL.md` v1.0 ACTIVE): three work commits C1 feature+add-peer / C2 clock+plumbing / C3 raw client + the §6 smokes + the fence test; grounded against live main (record_peer_url runtime.rs:464, set_clock :380, MockClock advance + new set_now, app.rs:680 startup install, xgen-mptest injector already holds the ws). The C2 trap, flagged: the clock verb advances a stashed concrete `Arc<MockClock>` handle, NOT a downcast of `runtime.clock()` (`Arc<dyn Clock>`), and does NOT pollute the `Clock` trait. **Next-active: Clair** — C1 → C2 → C3 → Chat doc-only close. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-313 → `tasks/M9_2_HARNESS_SEAMS_DESIGN.md` §3+§4 → `tasks/M9_2_HARNESS_SEAMS_AUDIT.md` §3+§4.** Clair picks up now (C1). Not pushed — Joe pushes.

Gates MP-A-12. **The unifying crux (M9.2-A4 — the FENCE):** all three widen what an external actor can make a node do (peer-spoof / clock-tamper / frame-fuzz), so the central question is the **fence, not the feature** — each seam real for the harness, provably absent from / inert in production (mirrors the M10 mock-module stance). **Forks M9.2-F-A..F-E:** F-A F2 shape (aicontrol `federation add-peer` and/or config peer-list; lean verb) · F-B F3 shape (aicontrol `clock advance/set` on the injected MockClock + ship the mock-clock build for the harness) · F-C F4 shape (`pub send_raw` on `Connection` vs rawness kept entirely inside `xgen-mptest`) · **F-D the FENCE = THE Joe-lock** (1 compile-time `harness-control` cargo feature [lean: physically un-buildable in release; composes with `mock-clock`] · 2 runtime dev-flag `--unsafe-harness-control` · 3 test-crate-only [F4 only]) · F-E grouping (one milestone; three small commits under one fence; F4 may need no production-crate change). **Honest boundary (D-065):** the fence's strength is the whole point — a runtime dev-flag means a misconfigured production deploy could expose these surfaces; a compile-time feature makes them un-buildable in release. A security property, named as such. Suite 1269/0/8 (no code). No DECISIONS change (M9.2-D# arc-local). ## 🟢 M9.1 — event timestamp-bound validation (F1/gap G6): design Joe-LOCKED (J-309, 2026-06-07) — future-skew ceiling on BOTH origins (convergence-safe by catch-up monotonicity); lands in `validate_event` (NOT the deprecated `validate_steps_8_13`); next-active = M9.1 runbook

**M9.1 design phase authored + Joe-LOCKED at J-309 (2026-06-07; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/M9_1_TIMESTAMP_VALIDATION_DESIGN.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Locks M9.1-D1..D5 (arc-local, D-069).** **Grounding correction (D-065 — supersedes audit §3.1 / F-D):** the audit + J-308 cite `validate_steps_8_13` at `xgen-node/src/message/exchange.rs`; live `main` says (1) the file is `xgen-core/src/message/exchange.rs` (Node runtime = `xgen-core/src/node/runtime.rs`; no `xgen-node/src/message/`), and (2) `validate_steps_8_13` is `#[deprecated]` (J-125) — only test fixtures + the deprecated `accept_event` call it, so patching it would NOT close the live hole. The production inbound gate is the F-4 unified core **`validate_event`** (exchange.rs:466), called live at **runtime.rs:1060** in `dispatch_event` (same class as the J-307 R1 `ingest_event`→`validate_event` correction). Grounded: `Event.timestamp: String` (RFC3339); `validate_event` is pure (no clock); at the live call site both `now` (= `self.clock.now_utc()`, D-090) and the event `origin`/`peer_node_id` are in scope; `validate_event` has exactly three call sites (def + one test + the one live caller) → threading a `now` param is a small touch. **Locks M9.1-D1..D5:** **D1** bound = future-skew ceiling ONLY (`timestamp > now + MAX_FUTURE_SKEW` ⇒ reject; far-past legitimate via catch-up + replay; unparseable rejected). Causal monotonicity (`timestamp ≥ max(prev.timestamp)`) considered + deferred (narrow marginal coverage; a strict `≥` rejects honest replies on a lagging clock → would need its own SKEW_TOLERANCE + tests; own small arc or M9.2 if wanted). **D2** (the crux, F-B) origin-gating = **BOTH origins**, NOT INV-EXP local-only. **D3** (F-D) where+how = a new "Step 8.5" inside `validate_event` (after step 8 / alongside step 10, before step 9/HeldPending), new `now: DateTime<Utc>` param, new `ExchangeError::TimestampOutOfBounds` (wire code assigned in the runbook with an explicit collision check — the 6009 lesson); MUST NOT feed `state_key_for_event`/resolver/ordering (D-076). **D4** (F-E) window = named const `MAX_FUTURE_SKEW`, default **5 min** (>> NTP skew, << a useful future-dating window; named, not tuned). **D5** (F-C) convergence safety gated by proof. **Convergence-safety claim (the load-bearing argument):** INV-EXP forced expiry gates local-only because invite-expiry is a PAST-deadline check (fixed `valid_until` vs increasing `now` → catch-up flips accept→reject → divergence). A FUTURE ceiling is the opposite shape: (1) monotone under catch-up — `now` only advances, so an event that passed at the home node passes more comfortably at any later peer, never flips accept→reject; (2) live-receipt disagreement is bounded by clock-skew δ, defeated by a 5-min margin >> δ. So the bound is convergence-safe on BOTH origins and CLOSES the federated injector (audit A5) instead of excusing it; a node skewed ahead by > the ceiling fails OPEN, never closed against honest traffic. **Proof plan (D5):** (a) `validate_event` unit, fixed `now` (=now/+4min accept; +10min reject; −30d accept; unparseable reject); (b) honest-skew same-verdict (two NodeRuntimes, δ≈2s, same +10min event → both reject); (c) catch-up leniency (event accepted live at A; same event at B with MockClock +2d → B accepts — the monotonicity property); (d) sensitivity witness (revert Step 8.5 → over-ceiling injector admitted, RED → would land in `.events`; restore → GREEN). MockClock already a xgen-core dev-dep (INV-EXP). **Honest boundary (D-065):** closes future-dated events from ANY origin (local liar / skewed local clock AND the federated injector, given future-direction skew); does NOT close PAST-dated events (indistinguishable from legitimate catch-up — no wall-clock gate can, that is D1's deferred monotonicity territory). **Runbook-grounding item:** confirm MP-A-15's skew direction — if future, D1 closes it (assert rejection + absence); if past, record out-of-scope + drive the witness with a synthetic future-skewed event. Suite 1262/0/8 (no code). No DECISIONS change (M9.1-D# arc-local). ROADMAP v2.97→v2.98. **Next-active: M9.1 runbook** (`tasks/M9_1_TIMESTAMP_VALIDATION_IMPL.md`) — Step 8.5 + error variant + wire-code collision check + caller wiring + the (a)–(d) tests → Clair (one commit + doc-only close); then M9.2 → Multiparty-tests. **Entry point (Rule 0): this PLAY → JOURNAL J-309 → `tasks/M9_1_TIMESTAMP_VALIDATION_DESIGN.md` §3+§4 → `tasks/M9_findings.md` (F1).** Clair stands down until the runbook exists. Not pushed — Joe pushes.

## 🟢 M9.1 — event timestamp-bound validation (F1/gap G6): Phase-0 OPENED (J-308, 2026-06-07) — protocol-fix sub-branch of M9; crux = origin-gating vs convergence; M9.2 staged PENDING; next-active = M9.1 design

**Clearing the five M9 findings before the Multiparty-tests milestone (Joe's "clean table" call), as two sub-branches of M9 (✅ CLOSED J-307, stays closed): M9.1 = the F1 protocol fix (this); M9.2 = harness-enablement seams F2+F3+F4 (test tooling, PENDING).** M9.1 Phase-0 OPENED at J-308 (2026-06-07; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/M9_1_TIMESTAMP_VALIDATION_AUDIT.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). **The defect (F1/gap G6):** `validate_steps_8_13` (xgen-node/src/message/exchange.rs, the F-4 13-step) checks event_id / causal / membership / signature but has NO timestamp bound → a skewed event is silently admitted (M9 injector MP-A-15). **Grounding:** D-076 makes ordering wire-order not timestamp, so the bound must be admission-only (never feed the resolver); reuse `self.clock.now_utc` (D-090) + the invite `valid_until` gates 3044/3045 + Trust Assertion `valid_until` precedent. **The crux (M9.1-F-B, the Joe-lock):** INV-EXP (J-298) taught that gates run iff `origin==LocallySubmitted` and skip on federation — because a local-clock-dependent check on every node could make A accept and B reject the same event → divergence; BUT the injector arrives as a peer (federation-class), so a local-only gate closes the local hole and NOT the federated injector. ## ⚫ M8.7 — concurrent-commit resolution (R only) CLOSED (J-302, 2026-06-07) — single commit shipped; `mls.commit` conflict domain + `mls_commit_tip` observable; convergence repro + sensitivity witness GREEN; next-active = Joe selects the next milestone (M9)

**M8.7 SHIPPED + CLOSED at J-302 (2026-06-07; Clair — single commit, code + 5 tests + doc-only close per D-074). Executes the J-300/J-301 Joe-LOCKED design (CC-D1..CC-D5). R only — crypto-agnostic, no openmls.** The last sequenced arc before M9. **The problem (A4):** `state_key_for_event` returned `None` for `MlsCommit`, so two members committing `N→N+1` at one frontier both applied and fold-order silently decided `mls_epoch`. **The fix (three small edits, one commit):** **Step 1** `RoomState.mls_commit_tip: Option<EventXgid>` (CC-D5 — the observable; after `mls_epoch` at state.rs:135; `None` at both constructors ~397/~789; genesis leaves `None`; rides the M8 `Eq` oracle additively; `RoomState` unserialized → **no persistence migration**); **Step 2** `apply_mls_commit` (state.rs:825) writes `room.mls_commit_tip = event.event_id.clone()` beside `mls_epoch` (D3-fencing comment corrected — race now resolved by the conflict domain); **Step 3** the `MlsCommit` arm in `state_key_for_event` (resolution/state_key.rs) — category `"state.mls_commit"`, key `"{room}:{target_epoch}"` (`content["epoch"].as_u64()?`; absent ⇒ `None`), **keyed by target epoch not per-Room** (CC-D2 regression guard: per-Room would let a later `2→3` compete with a losing `1→2` → epoch regression) + the stale `MlsGroupInit` + `_ => None` "mls.\*" comments corrected. **CC-D3 needed no `algorithm.rs` change** — `layer1_event_type_priority` returns early unless *all* events are membership events, so two `MlsCommit` events fall straight to Layer-5c lexicographic-by-`event_id` (verified by trace). **Why CC-D5:** the `mls_epoch` counter alone is **vacuous** — two honest commits both land `N+1` under either fold order, so a counter-only proof stays green with or without the fix; `mls_commit_tip` records *which* commit is canonical, the real divergence. **Tests (+5):** state_key units (same target_epoch share `room1:1` / differ / different room differ · **epoch-regression guard** target-epoch 2 vs 3 don't share · absent-epoch `None`) · resolution unit (frontier-2 → one lexicographic winner, single NodeRuntime) · **headline two-`NodeRuntime` convergence repro** (same two `1→2` commits ingested [A,B] vs [B,A], distinct node_ids, fixed `home_node` → both converge on `(mls_epoch, mls_commit_tip)` = the lexicographic winner via the `RoomState` `Eq` oracle) · **sensitivity witness** (revert the arm → both nodes agree `mls_epoch=Some(1)` but diverge on `mls_commit_tip` `dd20…`/`fc9a…` = RED; restore = GREEN — the vacuity CC-D5 defeats). **Checkpoint (the design-flagged verification, NOT a finding):** the live `ingest_event` SR-D1 gate applies the first-arriving commit transiently via the fast path, then the second commit's arrival makes `conflicts_in_log` true → `derive_resolved` rebuilds the whole snapshot folding **only the winner** (loser in `losers`, skipped) → converged tip is winner-only — **identical to membership conflict resolution** (a ban after a join applies the join transiently, then the ban rebuilds and excludes it); not an mls-specific defect; the two-Node repro is the order-independence proof. Surfaced precisely per D-065/D-084. **Honest boundary (D-065):** R proves convergence-on-winner (no permanent fork); loser rollback-and-replay is **L** (production openmls client rebuilds from the winner → epoch N+2) — the Arc H C1 Finding 1 analogue. S (real key schedule/HPKE) + home-DS serialization remain L (CC-D1/CC-D4). **Verify:** `cargo build --workspace --all-targets` 0; `cargo clippy --workspace --lib --tests -- -D warnings` clean on **default and `--all-features`**; `cargo test --workspace` **1212/0/2** (+5 over 1207). ## 🟢 M8.7 — concurrent-commit resolution: runbook authored + CC-D5 amendment (J-301, 2026-06-06) — single commit (mls_commit_tip + MlsCommit state-key arm), one checkpoint; Clair pickup

**M8.7 implementation runbook authored + Joe-approved at J-301 (2026-06-06; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_IMPL.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Clair may now pick up.** **CC-D5 design amendment (D-065 — caught while grounding the runbook):** the J-300 headline proof ("both nodes converge on the same `mls_epoch`") was **vacuous** — two honest concurrent commits both advance N→N+1, sharing `target_epoch`, so `mls_epoch` lands at N+1 under either fold order → the witness would stay GREEN with or without the fix (the theatre the M8.6 test-quality pass rejects). The real divergence is **which commit is canonical**, unobservable in Node state. **Fix:** `apply_mls_commit` records the resolved winner's id in a new `RoomState.mls_commit_tip: Option<EventXgid>` (beside `mls_epoch`); `derive_resolved` admits only the winner so tip=winner, loser excluded; the field rides `RoomState`'s existing `PartialEq`/`Eq` **M8 convergence oracle** additively; `RoomState` is `#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]` only (rebuilt from the log, runtime.rs:460/602), **not** `Serialize` → no persistence migration. Design bumped v1.0→v1.1. **Runbook (single commit, core change is small):** Step 1 `RoomState.mls_commit_tip` field (+ `None` at the ~397/~788 constructors; genesis `None`); Step 2 `apply_mls_commit` (state.rs:825) writes `room.mls_commit_tip = event.event_id.clone()` beside `mls_epoch` (D3-fencing comment corrected); Step 3 the `MlsCommit` arm in `state_key_for_event` — `category "state.mls_commit"`, `key_field "{room}:{target_epoch}"` (`content["epoch"]`, absent ⇒ `None`) + the stale `MlsGroupInit` epoch-advance comment & the `_ => None` "mls.\*" comment corrected; Step 4 tests; Step 5 checkpoint → close. **Tests:** state_key units (same target_epoch share / differ + **regression guard** `2→3` vs losing `1→2` do NOT share + absent-epoch None) · resolution unit (frontier-2 → one lexicographic winner) · headline two-`NodeRuntime` repro (two `1→2`, distinct nonces, ingested [A,B] vs [B,A] → both `RoomState`s `Eq` on `(mls_epoch, mls_commit_tip)` = the lexicographic winner) · **sensitivity witness** (revert arm → tips diverge RED, restore GREEN — the test a counter-only design would have left green). **Checkpoint (one, light):** final `apply_mls_commit`/field shape + the design-flagged verification that `apply_mls_commit` fires **only for the resolved winner** on the live path (pre-resolution apply → a finding, surface not work around — D-065/D-084) + suite green. **Scope:** R only (CC-D1), crypto-agnostic, no openmls. DoD has no "commit pushed" line. Untouched: `MlsGroupInit` key, genesis epoch, `mls.welcome`/`mls.proposal`, well-formedness validation, all crypto. Honest boundary at close: loser rollback-and-replay is L (convergence-on-winner only — Arc H C1 Finding 1 analogue). Suite **1207/0/2** (no code). No DECISIONS change (arc-local CC-D#, D-069). ROADMAP v2.89→v2.90. **Next-active: Clair** — Step 1 → … → checkpoint → close. **Entry point for Clair: this PLAY → JOURNAL J-301 → `tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_DESIGN.md` §3–§7 → `tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_IMPL.md` §2–§4 per Rule 0.** Not pushed — Joe pushes.

## 🟢 M8.7 — concurrent-commit resolution: design Joe-LOCKED (J-300, 2026-06-06) — scope corrected to R ONLY (S inseparable from L); MlsCommit conflict domain keyed (room, target_epoch) + Layer-5c lexicographic tiebreak; next-active = runbook

**M8.7 design phase authored + Joe-LOCKED at J-300 (2026-06-06; Chat Claude + Joe — design-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_DESIGN.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Four locks CC-D1..CC-D4.** **Scope correction (CC-D1, D-065 — supersedes audit §6 "R+S"):** grounding showed **S is inseparable from L** — the real MLS key schedule S would swap in is produced by the openmls group object (ratchet/secret tree), so S cannot ship without the production client. **M8.7 = R ONLY** (concurrent-commit convergence on the opaque Node-tracked `RoomState.mls_epoch`, a DAG-level property independent of the key schedule); **S folds into the L (production openmls-client) arc.** The home-DS commit serialization (the F-B "hybrid" half) is **L-side** (CC-D4): a live-delivery optimization (which winner online clients see first), NOT an R convergence requirement — the DAG tiebreak alone converges; F-B splits cleanly DAG-tiebreak=R / home-DS-serialize=L. **Problem (A4, grounded):** `state_key_for_event` returns `None` for `MlsCommit` (comment even claims epoch advances need no state key); `apply_mls_commit` (`state.rs:825`) sets `mls_epoch` unconditionally + self-fences the race to D3 → two members committing `N→N+1` at one frontier diverge by fold order on independent catch-up. **Mechanism (grounded — R adds NO new resolution machinery):** `derive_resolved` (`derive.rs:76`) groups by state key → causal frontier (`frontier_of`) → `resolve()` Layer-5c lexicographic-by-`event_id` winner (`derive.rs:36`) → winners fold into convergent `SpaceState`, losers excluded; node builds state from it (`runtime.rs:460/602`, cold-start convergent). Giving `MlsCommit` a state key IS the entire core change. **Fix — CC-D2 state key `(room, target_epoch)`:** `MlsCommit` arm → category `"state.mls_commit"`, key_field `"{room}:{epoch}"` (`target_epoch=content["epoch"]`; absent ⇒ `None`). **Load-bearing catch:** per-room keying would let a later `2→3` commit compete with a losing `1→2` commit (both frontier-concurrent) → tiebreak could pick the epoch-2 loser → **epoch regression**; keying by target epoch means only same-transition commits group, sequential advances never collide. **CC-D3 Layer-5c lexicographic, no Layer-1** (concurrent advances carry no semantic priority). `apply_mls_commit` logic unchanged (already winner-only on the resolved set); its D3-fencing comment + the stale `state_key.rs` comment corrected (D-065). **Change surface:** `resolution/state_key.rs` (arm + comment); `space/state.rs` (comment-only); one impl-time trace confirming `apply_mls_commit` fires only for the resolved winner. **Proof plan (in-process, no openmls client):** state_key units (same target_epoch share / differ; regression guard `2→3` vs losing `1→2` do NOT share) + resolution unit (frontier-2 → one deterministic winner) + headline two-`NodeRuntime` repro (two concurrent `1→2` → both nodes converge on same `mls_epoch=2` + same winning `event_id`, loser excluded on both) + sensitivity witness (revert arm → divergence RED, restore GREEN). **Coverage ledger / honest boundary (D-065):** R proves every node AGREES on the winner (no permanent fork); the loser's rollback-and-replay (rebuild from the winner, re-apply its proposal → epoch N+2) is **L** (real openmls client) — the in-process proof demonstrates convergence-on-winner, not loser-recovery (Arc H C1 Finding 1 analogue, named not glossed). S (real key schedule/HPKE) + home-DS serialization both deferred to L. Suite **1207/0/2** (design-only). No DECISIONS change (arc-local CC-D#, D-069). Audit v1.1→v1.2 (§6 supersede note). ROADMAP v2.88→v2.89 (M8.7 design Joe-LOCKED; R only). **Next-active: runbook** (`tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_IMPL.md`) — state-key arm + comment corrections + unit/resolution/two-Node/witness tests → Clair. **Entry point: this PLAY → JOURNAL J-300 → `tasks/M8_7_CONCURRENT_COMMIT_DESIGN.md` §3–§7 per Rule 0.** Clair stands down until the M8.7 runbook exists. Not pushed — Joe pushes.

ChaCha20 wrap → HPKE) is small + genuinely behind the interface; **(L)** real openmls lifecycle + production client is **net-new** (Phase-2 holds an epoch counter + one symmetric secret, NOT MLS group state — no ratchet tree / real Commit-Welcome gen; `ops::send` live-encrypt deferred per Arc H C1 Finding 1; client/multi-device-adjacent); **(R)** concurrent-commit resolution is **already self-fenced to D3 in code** (`apply_mls_commit`, `state.rs:810-824` — single-committer happy path; fold order silently decides `mls_epoch` under a race; no `state_key_for_event` arm) and is the milestone's center of gravity. **Forks framed (MLS-A1..A7; F-A..F-F).** **Joe-confirmed design leans (formal lock in the design phase, NOT here):** **F-B = hybrid** — the home node serializes commits as the DS (MLS-native ordering) + the DAG provides a deterministic tiebreak (epoch → conflict domain with a `state_key_for_event` arm; D-076 wire-order determinism already present); replicas trust the home's resolved order and do not re-adjudicate (the J-298 admission-only / F-5 / D-089 pairwise-trust shape); option 1 (openmls-native) needs a cross-node arbiter that fights the federation trust model; option 3 (single-committer) rejected as a capability regression. **Scope split** — **M8.7 = R + S** (resolution rule + conflict-domain wiring + primitive swap; protocol-complete; **in-process proof target: two committers at one frontier → both replicas converge on the same `mls_epoch` winner**, no live openmls client required), **L (production MLS client + loser rollback-and-replay + KeyPackage gen + Credential↔XGID + group-state persistence)** spun OUT to its own arc with the client/UI milestone — mirrors Arc H (ship substrate + in-process proof, defer production client). **R/L seam:** R decides the winning commit (core/node, provable in-process); L executes the loser's rebuild (real openmls client). **Honest risk flagged for the design coverage ledger (D-065):** without a real openmls client the in-process harness *simulates* loser-rebuild rather than exercising it (Arc H C1 Finding 1 analogue) — must be named, not glossed. ## ⚫ INV-EXP — invite-expiry replay-gate fix CLOSED (J-298, 2026-06-06) — origin-gated 3044/3045 (admission-only) + per-entry origin in PendingBuffer + 3044 clock→injected (D-090); two sensitivity witnesses; next-active = M8.7

**INV-EXP CLOSED at J-298 (2026-06-06; Clair — C1 + C2 + C3 + doc-only close).** The M8.6/C8 follow-on: the 3044 `invite_expired` join gate (and the 3045 over-ceiling invite gate) re-fired on **every** origin against the receiver's wall-clock, so a peer catching up a Space older than the invite's `valid_until` rejected the historical invited-join → membership diverged from the home node. **The fix (design §2):** the 3044/3045 gates are admission control — they run **iff `origin == LocallySubmitted`** and are **SKIPPED** (invite/join falls through to apply, NOT rejected) on `ReceivedViaFederation` (a replica trusts the home's already-made admission; F-5/D-089 pairwise trust). Made correct on the drain path by **per-entry origin in `PendingBuffer`**. **C1** (`xgen-core/src/dag/pending.rs`): `BufferedEntry.origin` (2nd `add()` param); `try_release` → `Option<(Event, EventOrigin)>`, `resolve`/`resolve_identity`/`resolve_federation_relationship` → `Vec<(Event, EventOrigin)>`; the three `drain_pending_*` re-dispatch each released event with its **stored** origin, batch param removed (callers updated: `dispatch_event` ×2, `app.rs` identity-replicate hook, `drain_pending_messages` + `RoomDag::drain_pending` tuple-destructure, ~9 test callers). RoomDag + legacy `accept_message` store `LocallySubmitted` (origin-blind drains). **C1 finding: none** (suite stayed green — at C1 the gate still ran on every origin and every drain-test's stored origin already equalled the old batch; behaviour-neutral at C1, load-bearing at C2). **C2** (`runtime.rs`): guard 3044 + 3045 on `LocallySubmitted` (skip ≠ reject, inline-commented); 3044 clock `Utc::now()` → `self.clock.now_utc()` (D-090; one-line local read, no borrow snag); PG-13 untouched. **C3** (6 tests in the runtime.rs INV-D6 module; `mock-clock` added to xgen-core `[dev-dependencies]`): headline two-`NodeRuntime` repro `inv_exp_federation_replay_preserves_membership` (aged-Space catch-up converges, deterministic via injected MockClock) + 4 per-path units + `inv_exp_mixed_origin_drain_preserves_per_entry_origin`. **Checkpoint #2 — two sensitivity witnesses recorded (file net-unchanged):** (1) C2 origin-guard revert → repro RED (B rejects the replayed join 3044), GREEN restored; (2) C1 per-entry-origin revert (fixed batch origin) → mixed-origin + local-buffered tests RED, GREEN restored. **Verify:** `cargo test --workspace` **1207/0/2** (+6); build all-targets 0; clippy `-D warnings` clean both feature sets. **Untouched (scope):** `ingest_event` restart path (IE-A2), drain `peer_node_id=None` F-3 approximation, PG-13; clock migration opportunistic (3044 only); MockClock test-only. **Below-the-lock (surfaced):** all six tests live in the runtime.rs INV-D6 module (helper-locality + xgen-core MockClock dev-dep) rather than a separate `node/tests/` file. **No DECISIONS change at close** (D-090 landed J-296; INV-D#/arc-local per D-069). ## 🟢 INV-EXP — design Joe-LOCKED (J-296, 2026-06-06) — origin-gate (3044/3045 admission-only) + per-entry origin in PendingBuffer + Clock migration (D-090); next-active = runbook

**INV-EXP design Joe-LOCKED at J-296 (2026-06-06; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code). Deliverable: `tasks/INV_EXP_REPLAY_GATE_DESIGN.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). That **rules out skip-on-drain** (would skip enforcement entirely, reopening the INV-D6 hole) and **forces per-entry origin**. **Locked fix:** run 3044 (and 3045, D-2) **iff origin == `LocallySubmitted`**, skip on `ReceivedViaFederation` (peer replicates an already-admitted fact; F-5/D-089 pairwise trust). Direct dispatch already origin-correct; the drain path is fixed by **per-entry origin in `PendingBuffer`** — `add()` gains `origin`, release fns return `Vec<(Event, EventOrigin)>`, drain loops re-dispatch with the **stored** origin (replacing the batch-`origin` call at runtime.rs:1573), batch param removed (forced because one drain releases a **mix** of origins). `peer_node_id=None`-on-drain F-3 approximation untouched. **Four-path correctness:** local-direct → gate runs · local-buffered→drained → runs at drain · fed-direct → skip · fed-buffered→drained → skip (bug fixed). **D-3:** 3044 clock migrates raw `Utc::now()` → injected `self.clock.now_utc()` → first cross-arc reuse → **D-090** (canonical injectable time source; opportunistic single-gate migration). **Tests:** headline repro `inv_exp_federation_replay_preserves_membership` (aged-Space catch-up dropped→converges, deterministic via injected Clock) + per-path units + mixed-origin drain test; xgen-core gains `mock-clock` dev-dep. ## 🟢 INV-EXP — invite-expiry replay-gate fix: Phase-0 OPENED (J-295, 2026-06-06) — 3044 gate re-fires on federation replay vs wall-clock; surfaced by M8.6/C8; next-active = design

**INV-EXP fix-arc Phase-0 OPENED at J-295 (2026-06-06; Chat Claude + Joe — doc-only, NO code, NO DECISIONS change). Deliverable: `tasks/INV_EXP_REPLAY_GATE_AUDIT.md` v1.0 (ACTIVE). Surfaced by M8.6/C8; Joe directed fixing it before M8.7; tree-child of M8.6 in ROADMAP.** **The bug (IE-A1):** the `3044 invite_expired` join gate (`xgen-core/src/node/runtime.rs:1208-1230`, in `dispatch_event`) checks `Utc::now() > invite.valid_until` on **every** origin. `valid_until` is absolute (14d default). On federation catch-up the invite replays (repopulates `pending_invites`), the join re-dispatches, and the gate compares the home-issued absolute deadline against the *receiver's current* wall-clock → a peer catching up a Space older than the invite window rejects the historical invited-join → that member is missing on the peer. **Scope:** **IE-A2** restart is SAFE (`replay_spaces_from_dir` → `ingest_event`, not `dispatch_event`) → federation-receive is the only affected surface; **IE-A3** 3045 over-ceiling is replay-stable (no `Utc::now()`), not a defect; **IE-A4** `drain_pending_by_identity(id, origin)` threads origin but `PendingBuffer` stores no per-entry origin → a buffered federation join drained under a local trigger gets the trigger's origin (fix must handle the drain path); **IE-A5** the fix signal already exists — `EventOrigin { LocallySubmitted, ReceivedViaFederation }` (runtime.rs:132-140); **IE-A6** the gate reads raw `Utc::now()`, not the injected Clock (outside M8.6's six-site fence) → extend the M8.6 Clock seam here for a deterministic repro — **first cross-arc `Clock` reuse → promotes it to a D-NNN.** **Design fork (framed, not locked):** Option A (origin-gate / admission-only) — run 3044 only at `LocallySubmitted`, skip on `ReceivedViaFederation` (a peer replicates an already-admitted DAG fact; aligned with F-5 Option-1 pairwise trust). Sub-decisions: **D-1** drain handling (resolve the upstream/downstream gate-vs-buffering ordering first), **D-2** 3045 admission-only (lean: yes), **D-3** Clock extension + promote (lean: yes). **Joe-lock to confirm in design:** *a peer trusts the home node's admission of a join and does not re-adjudicate invite-expiry on replication.* **Repro target:** aged-Space federation catch-up → member dropped on peer while present at home (needs IE-A6 clock injection). Suite **1201/0/2** (audit-only, no code). No DECISIONS change yet. ROADMAP v2.83→v2.84 (tree-child under M8.6; map NEXT-ACTIVE; chain before M8.7; M8.7 → [after INV-EXP]). **Next-active: design phase** — ground D-1, lock §6, author the design, then runbook → Clair. **Entry point: this PLAY → JOURNAL J-295 → `tasks/INV_EXP_REPLAY_GATE_AUDIT.md` §3–§6 per Rule 0.** Clair stands down until the runbook exists.
